- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`concordanceService.js`:

```js
/** One lookup, shared by the repetition panel (findRepetitions) and the claim check (suggestionFor). */
function repetitionLookup(conn) {
  const stmt = conn.prepare(
    `SELECT book, chapter, module_id, is_text
     FROM tm_segments
     WHERE en_norm = ? AND NOT (book = ? AND module_id = ?)
     ORDER BY (book = ?) DESC, applied_at DESC
     LIMIT 1`
  );
  return (book, moduleId, en) => {
    const enNorm = normalizeEn(en);
    if (!enNorm) return null;
    const m = stmt.get(enNorm, book, moduleId, book);
    return m ? { is_text: m.is_text, book: m.book, chapter: m.chapter, module_id: m.module_id } : null;
  };
}
```

`findRepetitions` keeps its body and its first-copy `seen` rule, calling `lookup(book, moduleId, seg.en)` instead of
`stmt.get`. `suggestionFor` loads the module (a load error returns `null`, as `findRepetitions` returns `[]`), and
returns the first copy of `segmentId` whose lookup matches, which is the copy `findRepetitions` would list.

`researchEmission.js` (CommonJS). Header: what the module is (spec §5.9–§5.11; the only path from a route to
`record`); why routes never call `record` themselves (decision 42); that nothing here may change a response (§5.11
step 3); that G1.23 replaces the two suggestion entries of `CLAIM_VALIDATORS` (§5.10).

```js
const log = require('../lib/logger');
const researchLog = require('./researchLog');
const concordance = require('./concordanceService');
const { sha256Text } = require('../lib/servedText');
const { ITEM_TYPES } = require('../lib/researchVocabulary');

const MAX_CLIENT_ACTION_ID = 128; // record stores a longer id as null (Task 11's buildEvent)
const MAX_ITEM_TEXT = 10000; // the save route's own editedContent cap (routes/segment-editor.js:354)
const SHA256_HEX = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
const ACCEPT_ORIGINS = Object.freeze(['single', 'keyboard-stream']);
/** The sources each item type may name; the client's own table is segment-save-state.js's ITEM_SOURCES. */
const ITEM_SOURCES = Object.freeze({
  'repetition-insert': Object.freeze(['tm']),
  'term-insert': Object.freeze(['termbase']),
  'markup-wrap': Object.freeze(['toolbar', 'keyboard']),
  'ai-suggestion': Object.freeze(['ai']),
  'rule-suggestion': Object.freeze(['rule']),
  'draft-restore': Object.freeze([null]),
});

const str = (v, max) => (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0 && v.length <= max ? v : null);
const int = (v, min = 0) => (Number.isSafeInteger(v) && v >= min ? v : null);

function readClientFields(body) {
  const b = body && typeof body === 'object' ? body : {};
  return {
    clientActionId: str(b.clientActionId, MAX_CLIENT_ACTION_ID),
    clientActionTime: int(b.clientActionTime, 1),
    replayed: b.replayed === true,
  };
}
const readAcceptOrigin = (body) =>
  body && ACCEPT_ORIGINS.includes(body.acceptOrigin) ? body.acceptOrigin : null;
const readReviewedEditSha256 = (body) =>
  body && typeof body.reviewedEditSha256 === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(body.reviewedEditSha256)
    ? body.reviewedEditSha256
    : null;

/** A whole-text item keeps the text it put in place (spec §5.10, §14 call 18) and the server's hash of it. */
function wholeText(d) {
  if (typeof d.text === 'string' && d.text.length <= MAX_ITEM_TEXT) return { text: d.text, textSha256: sha256Text(d.text) };
  if (typeof d.text === 'string') return { textLength: d.text.length, textDropped: true };
  if (d.textDropped === true) return { ...(int(d.textLength) !== null ? { textLength: d.textLength } : {}), textDropped: true };
  return {};
}
// tm_segments.chapter is TEXT (036-tm-segments.js:26; indexModule writes String(chapterNum)), so the client sends the
// suggestion's chapter as a string; a number is accepted too. Stored as a Number, like the events' own chapter.
const chapterOf = (v) =>
  Number.isSafeInteger(v) ? v : typeof v === 'string' && /^-?\d{1,4}$/.test(v) ? Number(v) : null;
const DETAIL = {
  'repetition-insert': (d) => ({
    ...wholeText(d), book: str(d.book, 64), chapter: chapterOf(d.chapter), moduleId: str(d.moduleId, 64),
  }),
  'draft-restore': (d) => ({ ...wholeText(d), ...(d.legacy === true ? { legacy: true } : {}) }),
  'term-insert': (d) => {
    const term = str(d.term, 200);
    return term === null ? {} : { term, termSha256: sha256Text(term) };
  },
  'markup-wrap': (d) => ({ marker: str(d.marker, 8) }),
  'ai-suggestion': () => ({}),
  'rule-suggestion': () => ({}),
};
const suggestionFields = (r) => ({
  opId: str(r.opId, 128), occurrence: int(r.occurrence), category: str(r.category, 64), runId: str(r.runId, 128),
  groupActionId: str(r.groupActionId, 128), rulingId: str(r.rulingId, 128), ruleId: str(r.ruleId, 128),
});

function validateRepetition(item, at) {
  if (typeof item.detail.textSha256 !== 'string') return 'unverified'; // the sent body carried no text
  const offered = at.offered(); // one module load per claimAssistItems call, not per item (Review Focus 39)
  if (!offered) return 'mismatch';
  return sha256Text(offered.is_text) === item.detail.textSha256 ? 'validated' : 'mismatch';
}
const unverified = () => 'unverified';
// Before G1.23 no suggestion is ever offered, so a claim to have accepted one is false (spec §5.10: "a failed check
// is mismatch"). G1.23 replaces these two entries with its §6 check.
const noSuggestionOffered = () => 'mismatch';
const CLAIM_VALIDATORS = Object.freeze({
  'repetition-insert': validateRepetition,
  'term-insert': unverified,
  'markup-wrap': unverified,
  'draft-restore': unverified,
  'ai-suggestion': noSuggestionOffered,
  'rule-suggestion': noSuggestionOffered,
});

function claimAssistItems(raw, place) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === null) return { items: [], droppedCount: 0 };
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return { items: [], droppedCount: 1 };
  // Every item of one save names the same segment, so the suggestion is looked up once. A throw is not memoized:
  // the next item asks again and is marked unverified the same way.
  let memo;
  const at = {
    ...place,
    offered: () =>
      memo !== undefined
        ? memo
        : (memo = concordance.suggestionFor(place.book, place.chapter, place.moduleId, place.segmentId)),
  };
  const items = [];
  let droppedCount = 0;
  for (const r of raw) {
    if (!r || typeof r !== 'object' || !ITEM_TYPES.includes(r.itemType)) {
      droppedCount += 1; // record throws on an unknown type, which would lose the whole event (decision 50)
      continue;
    }
    const src = r.source === undefined ? null : r.source;
    const item = {
      itemType: r.itemType,
      source: ITEM_SOURCES[r.itemType].includes(src) ? src : null,
      clientTimeMs: int(r.clientTimeMs),
      detail: DETAIL[r.itemType](r.detail && typeof r.detail === 'object' ? r.detail : {}),
      ...(r.itemType === 'ai-suggestion' || r.itemType === 'rule-suggestion' ? suggestionFields(r) : {}),
    };
    let claim;
    try {
      claim = CLAIM_VALIDATORS[r.itemType](item, at);
    } catch (err) {
      // The check could not run (a database error): an unverifiable claim is never called false.
      claim = 'unverified';
      log.warn({ errorCode: err.code || err.name, itemType: r.itemType }, 'Assist claim check failed; marked unverified');
    }
    items.push({ ...item, claim });
  }
  return { items, droppedCount };
}

module.exports = {
  ITEM_SOURCES, CLAIM_VALIDATORS, readClientFields, readAcceptOrigin, readReviewedEditSha256, claimAssistItems,
};
```

### Task 20: `emit` never throws, calls `record` bare, and counts a builder's failure

Spec §5.11 (steps 2, 3 and 6: the editing record first, a never-throw call, every loss countable); decisions 42, 14;
Review Focus 21.

**Files:**
- Modify: `server/services/researchEmission.js` — `emit(headFn, build)`, `headFor(req, eventType, place)`, the shared
  field sets `NO_CLIENT` and `NONE`
- Test: `server/__tests__/researchEmissionEmit.test.js` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `researchLog.record` (Task 11), through the module object (so a test can spy on it).
- Produces: `emit(headFn: () => Head, build: () => Fields | null): void` — never throws; `Head = { actorProviderId,
  eventType, book, chapter, moduleId }`; a `null` from `build` records nothing; a throw from `build` records `{ ...head,
  buildFailed: true }` (decision 14); a throw from `headFn` records nothing.
  `NO_CLIENT = { clientActionId: null, clientActionTime: null, replayed: false }` (decision 53: only save and accept
  events, and their `write-refused`, read the client's fields); `NONE` = every reference field `null` plus `NO_CLIENT`,
  for module-level events and requests that write several events. `headFor(req, eventType, { book, chapter,
  moduleId }) → Head`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Header: env before any server require (`SESSIONS_DB_PATH` in a `mkdtempSync` dir, `JWT_SECRET`), since
`researchEmission` requires `researchLog` and through it `researchDb`; then `E = require('../services/researchEmission')`,
`researchLog = require('../services/researchLog')`, `logger = require('../lib/logger')`, `SERVER = path.join(__dirname,
'..')`.

```js
const head = { actorProviderId: 'p-1', eventType: 'edit-save', book: 'efnafraedi-2e', chapter: 1, moduleId: 'm1' };
let recordSpy, errorSpy;
beforeEach(() => {
  recordSpy = vi.spyOn(researchLog, 'record').mockImplementation(() => ({ status: 'skipped', reason: 'not-consenting' }));
  errorSpy = vi.spyOn(logger, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
});
afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

it('a built event is recorded once, and the head wins over a builder that tries to set it', () => {
  E.emit(() => head, () => ({ segmentId: 's', actorProviderId: 'someone-else', eventType: 'apply' }));
  expect(recordSpy.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ ...head, segmentId: 's' }]]);
});
it('null from the builder records nothing (an act that wrote nothing)', () => {
  E.emit(() => head, () => null);
  expect(recordSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});
it('a builder that throws never throws out of emit; it is recorded as buildFailed, and the log holds no content', () => {
  const sentinel = 'SENTINEL-TEXT-' + 'x'.repeat(8);
  expect(() => E.emit(() => head, () => { throw new TypeError(sentinel); })).not.toThrow();
  expect(recordSpy.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ ...head, buildFailed: true }]]);
  expect(errorSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  const [obj] = errorSpy.mock.calls[0];
  expect(Object.keys(obj).sort()).toEqual(['book', 'errorCode', 'eventType']);
  expect(JSON.stringify(errorSpy.mock.calls)).not.toContain(sentinel);
});
it('a head that cannot be built records nothing and still does not throw', () => {
  expect(() => E.emit(() => { throw new Error('no req'); }, () => ({}))).not.toThrow();
  expect(recordSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});
it('control: a record that returns written is not looked at (routes ignore its result)', () => {
  recordSpy.mockImplementation(() => ({ status: 'written', eventId: 1n }));
  expect(E.emit(() => head, () => ({}))).toBeUndefined();
});
it('the source calls record outside any try (activityLogCallsiteGuard, Task 11)', () => {
  const src = fs.readFileSync(path.join(SERVER, 'services', 'researchEmission.js'), 'utf8');
  expect(src).not.toMatch(/try\s*\{\s*researchLog\.record\(/);
  expect(src.match(/researchLog\.record\(/g)).toHaveLength(1); // the one call site
});
it('the shared field sets: NO_CLIENT clears the client fields, NONE every reference field too', () => {
  expect(E.NO_CLIENT).toEqual({ clientActionId: null, clientActionTime: null, replayed: false });
  expect(E.NONE).toEqual({ segmentId: null, occurrence: null, servedVersionId: null, editingKind: null, editingId: null,
    editingContentSha256: null, origin: null, items: null, sessionId: null, ...E.NO_CLIENT });
  expect(Object.isFrozen(E.NONE) && Object.isFrozen(E.NO_CLIENT)).toBe(true);
});
it('headFor reads the actor from the token and the place from its argument', () => {
  expect(E.headFor({ user: { id: 'p-9' } }, 'apply', { book: 'b', chapter: -1, moduleId: 'm' }))
    .toEqual({ actorProviderId: 'p-9', eventType: 'apply', book: 'b', chapter: -1, moduleId: 'm' });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

Run: `npx vitest run server/__tests__/researchEmissionEmit.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — `E.emit is not a function`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```js
function headFor(req, eventType, { book, chapter, moduleId }) {
  return { actorProviderId: req.user.id, eventType, book, chapter, moduleId };
}

/**
 * The one call site of researchLog.record here (spec §5.11). Called by a route after its editing write committed and
 * before it answers. Never throws: a builder bug is recorded as a loss through record itself (decision 14), and record
 * is called after the try/catch, never inside it (it cannot throw; activityLogCallsiteGuard bans a try-wrapped call).
 */
function emit(headFn, build) {
  let head = null;
  let event = null;
  try {
    head = headFn();
    const fields = build();
    if (fields === null) return;
    event = { ...fields, ...head };
  } catch (err) {
    log.error(
      { errorCode: err.code || err.name, eventType: head ? head.eventType : null, book: head ? head.book : null },
      'Research event could not be built; recorded as a loss for a consenting actor (spec §5.11)'
    );
    if (!head) return; // with no actor and no place there is nothing record could count
    event = { ...head, buildFailed: true };
  }
  researchLog.record(event);
}
```

and the shared field sets (decision 53):

```js
const NO_CLIENT = Object.freeze({ clientActionId: null, clientActionTime: null, replayed: false });
const NONE = Object.freeze({
  segmentId: null, occurrence: null, servedVersionId: null, editingKind: null, editingId: null,
  editingContentSha256: null, origin: null, items: null, sessionId: null, ...NO_CLIENT,
});
```

Add `emit`, `headFor`, `NO_CLIENT` and `NONE` to `module.exports`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it**

Run: `npx vitest run server/__tests__/researchEmissionEmit.test.js server/__tests__/activityLogCallsiteGuard.test.js`
Expected: PASS.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add server/services/researchEmission.js server/__tests__/researchEmissionEmit.test.js
git commit -m "feat(server): emit — one never-throwing call from a route to record (G1.2)"
```

  `SEGMENT_CONFLICT` branch before its `return` and for a caught `err.refusal` before `sendRefusal`

  `itemsOf(eventId)`, `researchBytes()`, `withdrawConsent(userId)`
- Test: `server/__tests__/researchSaveEvents.test.js` (new)

**Interfaces:**

  `readClientFields`, `claimAssistItems` (Task 19, Task 20); `researchFixture.cjs` (Task 11).

  save response is unchanged, key for key. `emitRefused` stores a hash only when it is 64 lowercase hex (else `null`
  and `detail.echoMalformed: true`), and takes a save's echo from the body's `baseSha256` for the codes whose refusal
  carries none (`ECHO_FROM_BODY`, decision 49).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`server/__tests__/helpers/researchEventsFixture.cjs` (CJS; required after `SESSIONS_DB_PATH` is set):

```js
const fs = require('fs');
const { readResearch } = require('./researchFixture.cjs');
const { resolveResearchDbPath } = require('../../lib/researchDbPath');
const researchConsent = require('../../services/researchConsent');
/** Every research event in insertion-independent order: (server time, key, key_seq). Ids stay BigInt. */
function allEvents() {
  const db = readResearch();
  try {
    return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM research_events ORDER BY server_time_ms, editor_key, key_seq').all()
      .map((e) => ({ ...e, content: e.content_json && JSON.parse(e.content_json),
        before: e.before_json && JSON.parse(e.before_json), detail: e.detail_json && JSON.parse(e.detail_json) }));
  } finally { db.close(); }
}
const eventMark = () => new Set(allEvents().map((e) => String(e.id)));
const eventsSince = (mark) => allEvents().filter((e) => !mark.has(String(e.id)));
function itemsOf(eventId) {
  const db = readResearch();
  try { return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM research_event_items WHERE event_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal').all(eventId); }
  finally { db.close(); }
}
function researchBytes() {
  const { researchPath } = resolveResearchDbPath();
  return Buffer.concat([researchPath, `${researchPath}-wal`].filter((p) => fs.existsSync(p)).map((p) => fs.readFileSync(p)));
}
const withdrawConsent = (userId) => researchConsent.withdraw({ userId, nowMs: Date.now() });
module.exports = { allEvents, eventMark, eventsSince, itemsOf, researchBytes, withdrawConsent };
```

- `it('a saved text byte-identical to another editor\'s live edit is stored as its hash only (decision 65)')` —
  `p-ed2` saves `l` as `'SAMA-TEXTI'` (its own event keeps the text: no other editor holds it yet); `p-ed1` GETs `l`
  and saves exactly `'SAMA-TEXTI'` with `baseEditId`, the displayed pair (`p-ed2`'s row) and the served hash: 200; one
  `edit-save` whose `content` equals `{ category: null, note: null, textSha256: sha256Text('SAMA-TEXTI') }` (no `text`
  key) and whose `editing_content_sha256` is the same hash. Control, a different text keeps its text: `p-ed1` re-saves
  `l` as `'SAMA-TEXTI og meira'` with the current pair: one `edit-resave` whose `content.text` is `'SAMA-TEXTI og
  meira'`, while its `before[0].row` holds `edited_content_sha256: sha256Text('SAMA-TEXTI')` and no `edited_content`
  (the actor's own previous text, still byte-identical to `p-ed2`'s live edit), and keeps its own `created_at`.
- `it('a builder failure never fails the save, and is counted for a consenting editor (decision 14)')` — the route calls
  `servedEdit.saveOnServedBase` through the module object, so wrap the real one and return its result with one
  property the route never reads and the builder does:
  `const real = servedEdit.saveOnServedBase; vi.spyOn(servedEdit, 'saveOnServedBase').mockImplementation((input) =>
  Object.defineProperty({ ...real(input) }, 'supersededAcceptanceIds', { enumerable: true, get() { throw new
  TypeError('boom'); } }));` (restored in `finally`). Post a valid insert on `i`: 200, `Object.keys(body).sort()`
  equals `SAVE_KEYS`, the `segment_edits` row exists (the real save ran); `eventsSince(mark)` is `[]`; `losses()`
  gained exactly one row whose `interval_id === INT1`, `event_type 'edit-save'`, `error_class 'other'` (Task 12's
  `buildFailed` path); and `logger.error` was called with `{ errorCode: 'TypeError', eventType: 'edit-save', book:

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`researchEmission.js`:

```js
const SAVE_EVENT_TYPE = Object.freeze({ inserted: 'edit-save', updated: 'edit-resave', withdrawn: 'edit-withdraw' });
const clientOf = (req) => readClientFields(req.body);

function emitSave(req, { segmentId, editedContent, category, editorNote, saved }) {
  const eventType = SAVE_EVENT_TYPE[saved.outcome];
  if (!eventType) return; // 'unchanged': nothing was written (decision 51)
  emit(
    () => headFor(req, eventType, { book: req.params.book, chapter: req.chapterNum, moduleId: req.params.moduleId }),
    () => {
      const base = {
        segmentId, occurrence: saved.occurrence, editingKind: 'segment_edit', editingId: saved.editId,
        origin: 'editor-save', sessionId: null, ...clientOf(req),
      };
      if (saved.outcome === 'withdrawn') {
        return {
          ...base,
          servedVersionId: saved.before.served_version_id ?? null, // the deleted row's (spec §5.9 source table)
          editingContentSha256: sha256Text(saved.before.edited_content),
          content: null, before: { table: 'segment_edits', row: saved.before }, detail: null, items: null,
        };
      }
      const { items, droppedCount } = claimAssistItems(req.body && req.body.assists, {
        book: req.params.book, chapter: req.chapterNum, moduleId: req.params.moduleId, segmentId,
      });
      return {
        ...base,
        servedVersionId: saved.servedVersionId,
        editingContentSha256: saved.editedContentSha256,
        content: { text: editedContent, note: editorNote || null, category: category || null },
        before: saved.outcome === 'updated' ? { table: 'segment_edits', row: saved.before } : null,
        detail: {
          displayed: saved.displayed,
          supersededEditIds: saved.supersededEditIds,
          supersededAcceptanceIds: saved.supersededAcceptanceIds,
          ...(droppedCount ? { droppedAssistCount: droppedCount } : {}),
        },
        items,
      };
    }
  );
}

const MAX_SEGMENT_ID = 200;
const ECHO_FROM_BODY = new Set(['BOOK_NOT_MINTED', 'NO_LAYER', 'SEGMENT_NOT_FOUND', 'DUPLICATE_ID', 'STRUCTURE_BLOCKED']);
function emitRefused(req, { action, segmentId, refusal, origin = null }) {
  emit(
    () => headFor(req, 'write-refused', { book: req.params.book, chapter: req.chapterNum, moduleId: req.params.moduleId }),
    () => {
      const hash = (t) => (typeof t === 'string' ? sha256Text(t) : null);
      // detail is stored unfiltered (decision 49), and PR1 type-checks these strings but never their format, so a
      // value is kept only when it IS a hash; anything else is null and flagged, never stored (spec §5.6: no text).
      let malformed = false;
      const asHash = (v) => {
        if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null; // absent ('' is absent to PR1 too)
        if (typeof v === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(v)) return v;
        malformed = true;
        return null;
      };
      let echoed = 'echoedText' in refusal ? hash(refusal.echoedText) : refusal.echoedSha256;
      // These save refusals carry no echo on the refusal (PR1 Task 13's refuse() gets none for them); the client's
      // baseSha256 is the echo (decision 49). Never for STALE_DISPLAYED: its echo is the displayed hash, not the base.
      // Nor for SEGMENT_CONFLICT: the conflict check compared edit ids, never this hash.
      if (action === 'save' && ECHO_FROM_BODY.has(refusal.code) && req.body) echoed = req.body.baseSha256;
      const echoedSha256 = asHash(echoed);
      const currentSha256 = asHash('currentText' in refusal ? hash(refusal.currentText) : refusal.currentSha256);
      return {
        segmentId: typeof segmentId === 'string' && segmentId.length <= MAX_SEGMENT_ID ? segmentId : null,
        occurrence: null, servedVersionId: null, editingKind: null, editingId: null, editingContentSha256: null,
        origin, content: null, before: null, items: null, sessionId: null,
        detail: { reason: refusal.code, echoedSha256, currentSha256, action, ...(malformed ? { echoMalformed: true } : {}) },
        ...clientOf(req),
      };
    }
  );
}
```

The route (as PR1 Task 14 leaves it): `const researchEmission = require('../services/researchEmission');`. In the
`try`, immediately before `res.json({`:

```js
      // G1.2 (spec §5.9, §5.11): the research event, after the commit and before the response. It never throws and
      // never changes this response.
      researchEmission.emitSave(req, { segmentId, editedContent, category, editorNote, saved });
```

In the `catch`, the `SEGMENT_CONFLICT` branch (kept first, its answer unchanged) gains the emission before its
`return`:

```js
      if (err.code === 'SEGMENT_CONFLICT') {
        // Decision 49 ([USER] 2026-11-04, spec AMENDED 2026-11-04): the pre-G1.1 concurrency refusal is logged too.
        // It compares edit ids, not texts, so both hashes are null; the reason is the server's code, not the wire's.
        researchEmission.emitRefused(req, {
          action: 'save', segmentId, origin: 'editor-save',
          refusal: { code: 'SEGMENT_CONFLICT', echoedSha256: null, currentSha256: null },
        });
        return res.status(409).json({ error: 'conflict', message: err.message });
      }
```

and, after that branch and before `if (sendRefusal(res, err)) return;`:

```js
      if (err.refusal) {
        // spec §5.6: a refusal is logged with its reason and both hashes, never the text.
        researchEmission.emitRefused(req, { action: 'save', segmentId, refusal: err.refusal, origin: 'editor-save' });
      }
```

`ECHO_FROM_BODY` does not list `SEGMENT_CONFLICT` (its comment says why: nothing compared the body's hash). The
decision-65 case needs no code here: `record` withholds the text (Task 11), and this test pins it through the route.

Expected: PASS (the PR1 route suites run with no approved text listed, so `record` captures nothing there and their
assertions are untouched).

Expected: FAIL — `expected [] to have a length of 1` on every writing case, the four eligibility cases included; the

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```js
/** The accept's eligibility refusals (acceptanceService.acceptSegment, after PR1's placement). Decision 49: logged as
 * write-refused with no hash, since each compares row states, never a text. Pinned against the codes the service
 * throws (Task 22's test). */
const ACCEPT_ELIGIBILITY_REFUSALS = Object.freeze(['NO_TRANSLATION', 'EDIT_EXISTS', 'DISCUSS_OPEN', 'HUMAN_CONTENT']);

function emitAccept(req, { result }) {
  if (!result || result.alreadyAccepted) return; // spec §5.6: an alreadyAccepted result logs no event
  const a = result.acceptance;
  emit(
    () => headFor(req, 'mt-accept', { book: a.book, chapter: a.chapter, moduleId: a.module_id }),
    () => ({
      segmentId: a.segment_id, occurrence: a.occurrence ?? null, servedVersionId: a.served_version_id ?? null,
      editingKind: 'segment_acceptance', editingId: a.id, editingContentSha256: sha256Text(a.accepted_content),
      origin: null, content: null, before: null, items: null, sessionId: null,
      detail: { acceptOrigin: readAcceptOrigin(req.body) },
      ...clientOf(req),
    })
  );
}
```

The route: `let placed = null;` before the `try`; PR1's `const placed = servedEdit.placeOnServedBase(…)` becomes
`placed = …`; before `res.json({ success: true, ...result })`, `researchEmission.emitAccept(req, { result });`. The
catch, first:

```js
      if (err.refusal) {
        researchEmission.emitRefused(req, { action: 'accept', segmentId, refusal: err.refusal });
      } else if (err.code === 'STALE_CONTENT' && placed) {
        // spec §5.6: the accept's base check is the value check; its refusal is logged as one (decision 49).
        researchEmission.emitRefused(req, {
          action: 'accept', segmentId,
          refusal: { code: 'STALE_CONTENT', echoedText: acceptedContent, currentText: placed.seg.is },
        });
      } else if (researchEmission.ACCEPT_ELIGIBILITY_REFUSALS.includes(err.code)) {
        // Decision 49 ([USER] 2026-11-04, spec AMENDED 2026-11-04): an eligibility refusal is logged too, with no hash.
        researchEmission.emitRefused(req, {
          action: 'accept', segmentId, refusal: { code: err.code, echoedSha256: null, currentSha256: null },
        });
      }
```

then PR1's `if (sendRefusal(res, err)) return;` and the existing branches, unchanged. Add `ACCEPT_ELIGIBILITY_REFUSALS`
to `module.exports`.

- `it('revoke: an acceptance, its previous status; another\'s acceptance loses its time')` — the author accepts a

  acceptance's, `editing_content_sha256 sha256Text(accepted_content)`, `before[0]` has `table 'segment_acceptances'`,
  `row.status 'active'`, `row.accepted_at` present (own). The author accepts another; H revokes it: `before[0].row`
  has no `accepted_at`. Responses: `Object.keys(body).sort()` equals `['acceptance', 'success']`, no `before` in
  `body.acceptance`.
- `it('comment: length and hash only')` — H comments `'SENTINEL-COMMENT'` on an edit: 200 `{ success, commentId }`;
  one `edit-comment`, `content` equal to `{ comment: { length: [...'SENTINEL-COMMENT'].length, sha256: sha256Text('SENTINEL-COMMENT') } }`,
  `editing_id` the edit's; `researchBytes().includes('SENTINEL-COMMENT')` is `false`.
- `it('submit: the edit ids it stamped')` — the author submits `m97301`: response keys `['editedSegments',

  `detail` equal to `{ reviewId: body.reviewId, stampedEditIds: <SELECT id … WHERE review_id = body.reviewId ORDER BY

- `it('the responses never carry before (decision 44)')` — over every response body collected above,
  `keysDeep(body)` (from `researchBoundary.test.js`'s helper, copied or imported) contains neither `before` nor
  `writes`; control: `keysDeep({ x: { before: 1 } })` contains `before`. (Task 16 closed this; it is kept here as the
  regression pin for the routes this task edits.)
- `it('a reviewer act carries no client fields, even when the body sends some (decision 53)')` — H rejects an edit on
  `i` with `{ clientActionId: 'rv-1', clientActionTime: Date.now(), replayed: true }` in the body: the event's

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

Run: `npx vitest run server/__tests__/researchReviewEvents.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — `expected [] to have a length of 1` for every act (no emission). The response key sets and the
no-`before` pin pass already (Task 16).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```js
const REVIEW_DECISIONS = Object.freeze(['edit-approve', 'edit-reject', 'edit-discuss']);
const placeOf = (row) => ({ book: row.book, chapter: row.chapter, moduleId: row.module_id });
const editRef = (edit) => ({
  segmentId: edit.segment_id, occurrence: edit.occurrence ?? null, servedVersionId: edit.served_version_id ?? null,
  editingKind: 'segment_edit', editingId: edit.id, editingContentSha256: sha256Text(edit.edited_content),
  origin: edit.origin ?? null, items: null, sessionId: null,
});
const noteOf = (v) => (v === undefined || v === null || v === '' ? null : String(v)); // stored as `note || null`

function emitReview(req, eventType, { edit, before }) {
  emit(
    () => headFor(req, eventType, placeOf(edit)),
    () => {
      const ref = editRef(edit);
      if (!REVIEW_DECISIONS.includes(eventType)) {
        return { ...ref, content: null, before: { table: 'segment_edits', row: before }, detail: null, ...NO_CLIENT };
      }
      const echoed = readReviewedEditSha256(req.body);
      return {
        ...ref,
        content: { note: noteOf(req.body && req.body.note) },
        before: { table: 'segment_edits', row: before },
        detail: { reviewedEditSha256: echoed, mismatch: echoed !== ref.editingContentSha256 }, // never refused
        ...NO_CLIENT, // decision 53: a reviewer's act reads no client fields
      };
    }
  );
}
```

`emitDelete` (`edit-delete`, `editRef(before)`, `before: { table: 'segment_edits', row: before }`), `emitRevoke`
(`mt-accept-revoke`, acceptance fields as `emitAccept`, `before: { table: 'segment_acceptances', row: before }`),
`emitComment` (`edit-comment`, `editRef(edit)`, `content: { comment: typeof comment === 'string' ? comment :
String(comment) }`) and `emitSubmit` (`module-submit`, place from `req.params`/`req.chapterNum`, every reference field
null through `...NONE`, `detail: { reviewId, stampedEditIds }`) follow the same shape, each of the first three with
`...NO_CLIENT` (decision 53).

The routes (each as Task 16 leaves it):
- approve, reject, discuss: after `notifyDecision(edit, …)` and before `res.json({ success: true, edit })`,
  `researchEmission.emitReview(req, 'edit-approve', { edit, before: result.before });` (resp. `edit-reject`,
  `edit-discuss`).
- unapprove, return-to-pending: `researchEmission.emitReview(req, 'edit-unapprove', { edit, before: result.before })`
  (resp. `edit-return-to-pending`) **before** `res.json` (unapprove answers first today, `:1198`; its activity row stays
  after).
- delete: `segmentEditor.deleteSegmentEdit(editId, req.user.id);` becomes `const { before } =
  segmentEditor.deleteSegmentEdit(editId, req.user.id); researchEmission.emitDelete(req, { before });`, then
  `res.json({ success: true })`.
- revoke: `researchEmission.emitRevoke(req, { row, before: revoked.before });` right before `res.json({ success: true,
  acceptance: row })`; the activity row and the sidecar regeneration stay after.
- comment: `researchEmission.emitComment(req, { edit: result.edit, comment });` before `res.json({ success: true,
  commentId: result.id })`.
- submit: `researchEmission.emitSubmit(req, { reviewId: result.id, stampedEditIds: result.stampedEditIds });` before
  its `res.json` (whose fields stay explicit).

A comment at the first of these sites: *G1.2: emitted after the act committed and before the response (spec §5.11);
`before` is the research event's and never reaches the response (decision 44).*

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them**

Run: `npx vitest run server/__tests__/researchReviewEvents.test.js`, then one at a time
`server/__tests__/acceptanceRoutes.test.js`, `server/__tests__/crossBookAuthz.test.js`,
`server/__tests__/researchBoundary.test.js`, `server/__tests__/activityLogCallsiteGuard.test.js`
Expected: PASS.

```bash

git commit -m "feat(server): review, delete, revoke, comment and submit record their events; mismatch flagged, never refused (G1.2)"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```js
// NONE (Task 20): several events per request carry no client fields (decision 53)
const ids = (rows) => rows.map((r) => r.id);

function emitApply(req, { book, chapter, moduleId, before, trigger, reviewId = null }) {
  emit(
    () => headFor(req, 'apply', { book, chapter, moduleId }),
    () => ({
      ...NONE,
      content: null, // the per-row outcomes are in the detail (decision 48)
      before: [
        ...before.superseded.map((row) => ({ table: 'segment_edits', row })),
        ...before.selfHealReset.edits.map((row) => ({ table: 'segment_edits', row })),
        ...before.selfHealReset.acceptances.map((row) => ({ table: 'segment_acceptances', row })),
      ],
      detail: {
        trigger, ...(reviewId !== null ? { reviewId } : {}),
        applied: ids(before.applied), superseded: ids(before.superseded),
        supersededReviewerNote: {
          length: [...segmentEditor.SUPERSEDED_BY_NEWER_NOTE].length,
          sha256: sha256Text(segmentEditor.SUPERSEDED_BY_NEWER_NOTE),
        },
        selfHealReset: { editIds: ids(before.selfHealReset.edits), acceptanceIds: ids(before.selfHealReset.acceptances) },
        lapsedAcceptanceIds: before.lapsedAcceptanceIds,
        stampedAcceptanceIds: before.stampedAcceptanceIds, // the acceptances this apply published (decision 48)
      },
    })
  );
}
```

(`researchEmission.js` requires `./segmentEditorService` for the constant; it requires nothing back, so no cycle.)
`emitReviewComplete` reads the place inside its head function, from `segmentEditor.getModuleReviewWithEdits(reviewId)
.review`, and builds `content: { note: noteOf(notes) }`, `detail: { reviewId, status: result.status, apply }`.
`emitRestore`: place from the request, `content: { version }`, `detail: { lapsedAcceptanceIds: before ?
before.lapsedAcceptanceIds : [] }`.

The routes (each as Task 17 leaves it):
- apply: after `const result = withoutResearchKeys(applied);`, `researchEmission.emitApply(req, { book:
  req.params.book, chapter: req.chapterNum, moduleId: req.params.moduleId, before: applied.before, trigger: 'apply' });`
  before the activity row; `res.json({ success: true, ...result })` unchanged.
- apply-and-render: after `const applyResult = withoutResearchKeys(applyRaw);`, `emitApply(…, before: applyRaw.before,
  trigger: 'apply-and-render')`, before `runPipeline` (decision 57); `res.json({ …, applied: applyResult, … })`
  unchanged.
- review-complete: `let applyOutcome = 'not-attempted'; let applyBefore = null; let place = null;`; in the `approved`
  branch, after Task 17's `applied = withoutResearchKeys(appliedRaw);`, `applyBefore = appliedRaw.before; applyOutcome
  = 'applied'; place = review.review;`; in the catch, `applyOutcome =
  applyErr.code === 'APPLY_HELD' ? 'held' : 'failed';` beside the existing `applied =` assignments. Before
  `res.json`:

  ```js
      const reviewId = parseInt(req.params.reviewId, 10);
      researchEmission.emitReviewComplete(req, { reviewId, result, notes: req.body?.notes, apply: applyOutcome });
      if (applyBefore) {
        researchEmission.emitApply(req, { book: place.book, chapter: place.chapter, moduleId: place.module_id,
          before: applyBefore, trigger: 'review-complete', reviewId });
      }
  ```

  The response and the activity row after it are unchanged (the row's `result.book` stays as it is, Review Focus 34).
- restore: after `const result = withoutResearchKeys(restored);`, `researchEmission.emitRestore(req, { version, before:
  restored.before });`, then `res.json({ success: true, ...result })` unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`researchEmission.js`:

```js
function emitPropagation(req, { sourceSegmentId, sourceIs, latest, editedContent, category, note, writes, skipped }) {
  writes.forEach((w, index) => {
    emit(
      () => headFor(req, 'propagation', { book: req.params.book, chapter: w.chapter, moduleId: w.moduleId }),
      () => {
        const clientTextSha256 = sha256Text(editedContent);
        let previewSource = 'base';
        let previewText = sourceIs;
        if (latest) {
          previewSource = String(latest.editor_id) === String(req.user.id) ? 'own-edit' : 'other-edit';
          previewText = latest.edited_content;
        }
        const previewSha256 = sha256Text(previewText);
        return {
          ...NONE, // no clientActionId: one request, several events (decision 53)
          segmentId: w.segmentId, occurrence: w.occurrence, servedVersionId: w.servedVersionId,
          editingKind: 'segment_edit', editingId: w.editId, editingContentSha256: clientTextSha256, origin: 'propagation',
          content: { text: editedContent, note: noteOf(note), category: category || null }, // the text written, once
          before: w.inPlace ? { table: 'segment_edits', row: w.before } : null,
          detail: {
            source: { moduleId: req.params.moduleId, segmentId: sourceSegmentId },
            clientTextSha256, previewSha256, previewSource,
            ...(previewSource === 'other-edit' ? {} : { previewText }), // another editor's text only as a hash (R1)
            previewMismatch: clientTextSha256 !== previewSha256,
            supersededAcceptanceIds: w.supersededAcceptanceIds,
            batch: { index, size: writes.length },
            ...(index === 0 ? { skipped } : {}),
          },
        };
      }
    );
  });
}
```

The route (as PR1 Task 16 leaves it): PR1's organic branch gains, before its `return res.status(409)…`,
`researchEmission.emitRefused(req, { action: 'propagate', segmentId, refusal: { code: 'BOOK_NOT_MINTED',
echoedSha256: null, currentSha256: null }, origin: 'propagation' });`. After `findOccurrences`:

```js
        sourceSegmentId: segmentId, sourceIs: sourceSeg?.is ?? '', latest, editedContent, category,
        note: note || 'Sjálfvirk fjölgun', writes: created.writes, skipped: created.skipped,
      });
      res.json(result);
```

### Task 29: T11 — the editing record rebuilt from the events

Spec §10 **T11** (whole: the acts, the field list, keys mapped, timestamps only as order, claims, the dropped-event
control), §5.9, §5.10; decisions 48, 50, 51, 60, 65 (a withheld text resolved by its hash); Review Focus 44.

**Files:**
- Create: `server/__tests__/helpers/researchRebuild.cjs` — `rebuildSegmentEdits(events, keyItems)`,
  `actualSegmentEdits(db, keyOfProvider)`, `diffRebuild(rebuilt, actual)`
- Test: `server/__tests__/researchReplay.test.js` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every emitting task; `researchEventsFixture.allEvents` (Task 21); `researchFixture.readResearch` (Task 11);
  `segmentEditor.SUPERSEDED_BY_NEWER_NOTE` (Task 17); `sha256Text` (PR1 Task 1).
- Produces: the rebuild helper (Task 30 reuses it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the helper and the test**

`researchRebuild.cjs`:

```js
const { sha256Text } = require('../../lib/servedText');
const FIELDS = ['book', 'chapter', 'module_id', 'segment_id', 'occurrence', 'edited_content', 'editor_note_sha256',
  'reviewer_note_sha256', 'category', 'status', 'applied', 'served_version_id', 'origin', 'editor_key', 'reviewer_key'];
const n = (v) => (v === null || v === undefined ? null : Number(v)); // sessions.db ids: safe as Number
const noteSha = (c) => (c && c.note ? c.note.sha256 : null);
/** events: allEvents() order (server time, then key_seq). Returns Map<editId, row-projection>. */
function rebuildSegmentEdits(events) {
  // Decision 65: a text another editor held live is stored as content.textSha256. It is resolved from any event in
  // the file whose content carries that text (the other editor's own save, if they consented); a text no event
  // carries (a decliner's) stays { sha256 }, which diffRebuild compares by hash.
  const textByHash = new Map();
  for (const e of events) {
    if (e.content && typeof e.content.text === 'string') textByHash.set(sha256Text(e.content.text), e.content.text);
  }
  const textOf = (c) =>
    typeof c.text === 'string' ? c.text : textByHash.get(c.textSha256) ?? { sha256: c.textSha256 };
  const rows = new Map();
  for (const e of events) {
    const id = n(e.editing_id);
    const at = { book: e.book, chapter: n(e.chapter), module_id: e.module_id, segment_id: e.segment_id, occurrence: n(e.occurrence) };
    const write = (r) => Object.assign(r, { edited_content: textOf(e.content), editor_note_sha256: noteSha(e.content),
      category: e.content.category ?? null, served_version_id: n(e.served_version_id), origin: e.origin });
    // A row whose creating event is missing (the dropped-event control) is skipped, never invented: the
    // difference must show up in diffRebuild, not as a TypeError here.
    const set = (rid, fields) => { const r = rows.get(n(rid)); if (r) Object.assign(r, fields); };
    switch (e.event_type) {
      case 'edit-save':
        rows.set(id, write({ ...at, status: 'pending', applied: false, reviewer_note_sha256: null,
          editor_key: e.editor_key, reviewer_key: null }));
        for (const s of e.detail.supersededEditIds) set(s, { status: 'superseded' });
        break;
      case 'edit-resave':
        if (rows.has(id)) write(rows.get(id));
        for (const s of e.detail.supersededEditIds) set(s, { status: 'superseded' });
        break;
      case 'propagation':
        if (!e.before) rows.set(id, write({ ...at, status: 'pending', applied: false, reviewer_note_sha256: null,
          editor_key: e.editor_key, reviewer_key: null }));
        else if (rows.has(id)) write(rows.get(id));
        break;
      case 'edit-withdraw': case 'edit-delete': rows.delete(id); break;
      case 'edit-approve': case 'edit-reject': case 'edit-discuss':
        set(id, { status: { 'edit-approve': 'approved', 'edit-reject': 'rejected', 'edit-discuss': 'discuss' }[e.event_type],
          reviewer_key: e.editor_key, reviewer_note_sha256: noteSha(e.content) });
        break;
      case 'edit-unapprove': case 'edit-return-to-pending':
        set(id, { status: 'pending', reviewer_key: null, reviewer_note_sha256: null });
        break;
      case 'apply':
        for (const a of e.detail.selfHealReset.editIds) set(a, { applied: false });
        for (const a of e.detail.applied) set(a, { applied: true });
        for (const s of e.detail.superseded)
          set(s, { status: 'superseded', applied: true, reviewer_note_sha256: e.detail.supersededReviewerNote.sha256 });
        break;
      default: break; // module-level and acceptance events do not change segment_edits
    }
  }
  return rows;
}
```

`actualSegmentEdits(db, keyOfProvider)` projects `SELECT * FROM segment_edits` onto `FIELDS` (`editor_note_sha256 =
editor_note === null ? null : sha256Text(editor_note)`, likewise the reviewer's; `applied = applied_at !== null`;
`editor_key = keyOfProvider(editor_id)`, `reviewer_key = reviewer_id === null ? null : keyOfProvider(reviewer_id)`)
into a `Map` by id. `diffRebuild(a, b)` returns `[{ id, field, rebuilt, actual }]` over the union of ids (a missing
row is `{ id, field: '<row>' }`), sorted; a rebuilt `edited_content` of the form `{ sha256 }` equals an actual text
whose `sha256Text` is that hash (decision 65's unresolvable case; every other field compares by value).
`keyOfProvider(p)` = `userService.findByProviderId(p).id` → that user's interval from `research_consent` (the starting
row's id) → `SELECT editor_key FROM editor_keys WHERE interval_id = ?` on `readResearch()`; it returns `null` when the
user has no interval (a decliner, an undecided editor) or the interval no key, which is how a decliner's identity
column maps "through the keys" (Task 30).

`researchReplay.test.js` — setup as Task 24's (the three stubs, the hold stub of decision 60, the pipeline stubs not
needed); `vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] })` with `act(fn)` = `vi.setSystemTime(T0 + ++step); return fn();` so
every event has its own `server_time_ms`. Book `efnafraedi-2e` in the temp root: `m99001` (MT: `a` EN `Recurring
sentence.`, `b`, `c`, `d` EN `Spread me.`, `e`, `f`), `m99002` (`t` EN `Spread me.`), `m99003` (`u` EN `Spread me.`),
`m99009` (faithful: `r` EN `Recurring sentence.` IS `Samþykkt þýðing.`, indexed). Users `p-a`, `p-b` (editors),
`p-h` (head editor); every one consents.

The acts, in order (each through its real handler, each wrapped in `act`):
1. `p-a` saves `a` (`'A1'`, note `'na'`) with assists: the real repetition insert (`'Samþykkt þýðing.'`), a planted one
   (`'Fölsuð.'`), a term insert, a wrap, and a repetition insert whose text the sent body dropped
   (`textDropped: true`).
2. `p-a` re-saves `a` (`'A1b'`, note `'nb'`: the re-save's `UPDATE` sets `editor_note = editorNote || null`,
   `segmentEditorService.js:145-151`, so without a note here no row a save wrote would end with a note).
3. `p-a` saves `b` (`'B?'`), then withdraws it (saves `b`'s base).
4. `p-b` saves `c` (`'C1'`). Then `p-b` saves `f` (`'F-sameiginlegt'`), and `p-a` saves `f` with exactly
   `'F-sameiginlegt'` (`baseEditId` and the displayed pair of `p-b`'s row): a copy of another editor's live edit, so
   `p-a`'s `edit-save` stores only `textSha256` (decision 65). Both `f` rows stay pending to the end (no act reviews
   them, and an apply touches only approved rows).
5. `p-h` approves `a`; unapproves it; rejects it; returns it to pending; approves it again (no note on any of them).
6. `p-h` marks `c` for discussion (note `'rædum'`); returns it to pending (which clears the reviewer fields,
   `segmentEditorService.js:566-575`); approves it with note `'samþ.'` (the one reviewer note a review event leaves on
   a row to the end: act 11 applies `c` as a winner and writes no note over it).
   **Then wait 1.1 s of real time** (`await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100))`; only `Date` is faked, and
   SQLite's `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` reads the real clock at second resolution), so the rows written after this point have
   a later `created_at` than those before it and the order test below compares at least one pair.
7. `p-a` saves `e` (`'E1'`); `p-h` rejects it (no note); `p-a` saves `e` again (`'E2'`: an insert whose sweep
   supersedes the rejected row).
8. `p-a` saves `m99003:para:u` (`'U-mitt'`: an own pending row on a propagation target).
9. `p-a` propagates from `m99001:para:d` with `'Fjölgað'`: `t` inserted, `u` updated in place.
10. `p-b` saves `a` (`'Ab-nýrra'`, newer than `p-a`'s approved row); `p-h` approves it.
11. `p-h` applies `m99001` (winners `p-b`'s `a` and `c`; loser `p-a`'s `a`).
12. Delete the temp faithful file; `p-h` applies `m99001` again (the self-heal).

- `it('T11: the rebuild from the events equals segment_edits on the spec\'s fields')` —
  `expect(diffRebuild(rebuildSegmentEdits(allEvents()), actualSegmentEdits(db, keyOfProvider))).toEqual([])`, and the
  rebuilt map holds every row id `SELECT id FROM segment_edits` holds (the union check inside `diffRebuild` makes a
  missing row a difference). **Positive controls, so the note fields are compared on real values, not on `null` both
  sides:** in the actual projection, `p-a`'s `a` row (last write event `edit-resave`) has `editor_note_sha256 ===
  sha256Text('nb')`, and `c` has `reviewer_note_sha256 === sha256Text('samþ.')`, set by an `edit-approve` (not by the
  apply, whose fixed note lands only on losers). **And the withheld text was resolved, not compared by hash:** `p-a`'s
  `f` event has no `content.text` and `content.textSha256 === sha256Text('F-sameiginlegt')`, while the rebuilt `f` row
  of `p-a` holds the string `'F-sameiginlegt'` (taken from `p-b`'s `edit-save`), not `{ sha256 }`.
- `it('T11: timestamps agree as order')` — for every pair of actual rows whose `created_at` differ, the row with the
  earlier `created_at` has the earlier last write event (`edit-save`, `edit-resave` or `propagation`) in
  `allEvents()` order (second-resolution `created_at` ties are not compared); and the number of compared pairs is
  above 0 (the control that makes the check non-vacuous: without act 6's wait every row can share one second).
- `it('T11: claims — planted mismatch, real validated, untestable unverified')` — the first `edit-save`'s items, by
  ordinal: `[['repetition-insert', 'validated'], ['repetition-insert', 'mismatch'], ['term-insert', 'unverified'],
  ['markup-wrap', 'unverified'], ['repetition-insert', 'unverified']]`.
- `it('control: dropping one event makes the rebuild differ, at exactly the row and field it changed')` — drop the
  `edit-reject` of `p-a`'s first `e` row (act 7; found by `event_type` and `editing_id`): the later sweep still
  supersedes that row, so the only field the drop leaves wrong is the reviewer:
  `diffRebuild(rebuildSegmentEdits(withoutIt), actual)` equals `[{ id: <e1>, field: 'reviewer_key', rebuilt: null,
  actual: keyOfProvider('p-h') }]` (the reject carried no note, so `reviewer_note_sha256` is `null` on both sides).
  A second drop, of `p-b`'s `edit-save` of `c` (act 4), gives exactly `[{ id: <c>, field: '<row>' }]` (the row is
  missing from the rebuild, and the later events on it are skipped, not invented).

Run: `npx vitest run server/__tests__/researchReplay.test.js`

## Interfaces produced for later PRs

CommonJS under `server/`; tests reach them through `createRequire(import.meta.url)`. PR4 builds deletion, retention,
the backups and the admin reader on these; PR5 builds the end-of-editing export and `opening-eligibility.js`; G1.23
calls `record` and writes `book_switch_events`.

### `server/services/researchLog.js` (Tasks 7, 11, 12) — the one call, and the key

```ts
record(event: {
  actorProviderId: string,          // req.user.id: the token's sub (the provider id), NOT users.id (decision 8)
  eventType: EventType,             // researchVocabulary.EVENT_TYPES
  book: string, chapter: number,    // chapter -1 = appendices
  moduleId: string,
  segmentId?: string | null, occurrence?: number | null,
  servedVersionId?: number | null,  // spec §5.9's source table; record looks base_is_sha256 up itself
  editingKind?: EditingKind | null, editingId?: number | null,   // set together
  editingContentSha256?: string | null,   // 64 lowercase hex: sha256Text of the editing row's text then (decision 7)
  origin?: EditOrigin | ReservedEditOrigin | null,               // PR1's editOrigins.js
  content?: { text?: string, note?: string | null, comment?: string | null, category?: string | null,
              version?: number | string } | null,
  before?: BeforeEntry | BeforeEntry[] | null,   // BeforeEntry = { table: 'segment_edits' | 'segment_acceptances', row }
  detail?: object | null,           // JSON-serialisable; never another person's text (propagation: preview text only
                                    // for previewSource 'own-edit' or 'base'; record drops it when another editor
                                    // holds the same text live, decision 65)
  items?: Item[] | null,            // only on edit-save, edit-resave, suggestion-reject, suggestion-left-open
  clientActionId?: string | null,   // 1–128 chars, else stored as null (no dedupe)
  clientActionTime?: number | null, // client ms; required for a replay to be captured (decision 16)
  replayed?: boolean,               // true only for a saveRetry replay
  sessionId?: string | null,        // NULL until G1.3
  buildFailed?: true,               // emit's builder threw: an 'other' loss for a consenting actor (decision 14)
}): { status: 'written', eventId: bigint } | { status: 'duplicate' }
  | { status: 'skipped', reason: 'not-study' | 'no-user' | 'not-consenting' | 'before-consent' }
  | { status: 'lost', reason: 'unavailable' | 'busy' | 'constraint' | 'consent-unknown' | 'other', ledgered: boolean }
// Never throws. Call it bare (activityLogCallsiteGuard bans a try-wrapped call), synchronously, AFTER the editing
// write committed, BEFORE the response; ignore its result.

type Item = { itemType: ItemType, source?: string | null, clientTimeMs?: number | null, detail?: object | null,
              claim: 'validated' | 'unverified' | 'mismatch',     // set by the caller's check (§5.10; Task 19)
              opId?, occurrence?, category?, runId?, groupActionId?, rulingId?, ruleId? }   // G1.23's suggestion fields
ensureKey(intervalId: number): { status: 'minted' | 'exists' | 'failed' }   // never throws; never returns the key
consentLookupFailing(): boolean                                               // for /api/health only
_reset(): void                                                                // test-only
// Object.keys(module) is exactly ['_reset', 'consentLookupFailing', 'ensureKey', 'record'] (decision 40)
```

**What `record` does to each input** (so no caller filters for it, and none may pre-filter differently):
- `content.text` is stored **verbatim**: pass it only when the actor wrote it (§5.9); a reviewer's event passes no
  text and carries the edit's hash in `editingContentSha256`. **Decision 65:** when another editor holds the same
  bytes as a live edit (status not `rejected` or `superseded`) on the event's segment, or on a propagation's
  `detail.source` segment, `record` stores `content.textSha256` instead; the caller passes the text either way and
  never `textSha256` itself. `note` and `comment` are always reduced to `{ length, sha256 }` (code points;
  `sha256Text`); `''` and `null` become `null`. Any other content key fails the build (an `other` loss): pass
  `category` and `version` only as named (decision 11).
- `before`: pass the **raw prior rows** (`SELECT *` inside the write's transaction); `record` applies decisions 8–10
  field by field: locators and state kept; `applied_at` → an `applied` boolean; `edited_content` kept only when
  `editor_id` is the actor and no other editor holds the same text live on a segment the event names (decision 65),
  else `edited_content_sha256`; free text reduced; `original_content`, `accepted_content`, `review_id`, `superseded_at`
  dropped; every identity column dropped (decision 9, approved 2026-11-04); `created_at` /
  `reviewed_at` / `accepted_at` kept only when the matching identity column is the actor. Stored as an array of
  `{ table, row }`. A column the classification table does not name is dropped at run time.
- `items`: mapped one row each, `ordinal` in list order, `outcome` from the event type, `client_time_ms` truncated,
  `detail` as canonical JSON (whole-text items keep their text there, §5.10). An unknown `itemType`, `claim` or key
  fails the build: **validate the client's list first** (Task 19's `claimAssistItems`), never pass a client string as
  `itemType`.
- `detail` is stored as canonical JSON, unfiltered but for decision 65 (`researchFilter.DETAIL_TEXT_KEYS`: a
  propagation's `previewText` is dropped when another editor holds it live), so a caller passes only what it has
  checked. `write-refused`: `detail = { reason, echoedSha256, currentSha256, action, echoMalformed? }` (decision 49),
  each hash 64 lowercase hex or `null` (both `null` for `SEGMENT_CONFLICT` and the accept's eligibility refusals), no
  content.

### `server/lib/researchVocabulary.js`, `researchFilter.js`, `canonicalJson.js` (Tasks 8, 10)

`EVENT_TYPES`, `EDITING_KINDS`, `ITEM_TYPES`, `ITEM_CLAIMS`, `ITEM_OUTCOME_BY_EVENT`, `LOSS_ERROR_CLASSES`: frozen, exact
values in Task 10 Step 1. A new event type (G1.3, a later surface) is added there with its test. `researchFilter`:
`BEFORE_FIELD_CLASSES` (drift-guarded against `PRAGMA table_info`: a new editing column fails Task 10's guard until it
is classified), `DETAIL_TEXT_KEYS`, `freeText`, `filterContent`, `filterBefore`, `filterDetail` (each of the three
filters takes `{ withheld }`, decision 65's set, which only `record` computes), `buildItemRows`. `canonicalJson(v)`:
keys sorted at every depth.

### `server/services/researchConsent.js` (Task 5) — for the routes, PR4's paper script and withdrawal

```ts
decisionStateFor(userId: number): { state: 'undecided'|'consented'|'declined'|'withdrawn', decidedTextVersion: string|null }
recordDecision({ userId, decision: 'consented'|'declined', text: { version, sha256 }, nowMs }):
  { outcome: 'started'|'renewed'|'unchanged'|'withdrew'|'declined', intervalId: number|null }
  // throws on an unapproved text; on 'withdrew', intervalId is the withdrawn interval's id (PR4 deletes its rows)
withdraw({ userId, nowMs }): { withdrawn: boolean, intervalId: number|null }    // PR4 adds the immediate deletion after it
captureStateFor(userId): { capturing: false } | { capturing: true, intervalId, startedAtMs }   // throws on a failed read
anyConsentedRow(): boolean
recordLoss({ intervalId, book, chapter, moduleId, eventType, errorClass, clientActionId, nowMs }): boolean   // never throws
getSessionsDb(): Database
```

**The interval rule** (spec §5.14, decisions 15, 27, 28): the current decision is the user's highest-`id` row; an
interval is live while its starting row (`id === interval_id`) has no `withdrawn_at_ms`; a `consented` decision on a
new text during a live interval inserts a row carrying the live interval's id; declining during a live interval sets
`withdrawn_at_ms` on the starting row and inserts the `declined` row, in one transaction; capture also requires the
latest row's text to be on the approved list. PR4's paper-consent script calls `recordDecision` / `withdraw` with an
approved text (§5.14), and a `started` outcome is followed by `researchLog.ensureKey`.

### `server/services/researchDb.js` and the schema (Tasks 1–3, 6)

```ts
BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS: 100; RETRY_INTERVAL_MS: 60000                // enforceable values, pinned by Task 6's test
getHandle(): Database               // WAL, defaultSafeIntegers(true); throws { code: 'RESEARCH_UNAVAILABLE', reason }
_reset(); _setTestPaths(p | null)   // test-only; _setTestPaths resets first, so an open handle never hides new paths
openAtBoot(): { ok: true } | { ok: false, reason }
status(): { state, reason, lastAttemptAtMs, sticky }
noteFailure(err): void
deriveResearchPath(p), resolveResearchDbPath() → { researchPath, editorialPath }        // server/lib/researchDbPath.js
RESEARCH_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1; ensureResearchSchema(db) → { ok, version } | { ok: false, code, error }   // never throws
```

- **A later column is a new schema step** (`STEPS` in `server/lib/researchSchema.js`, gated by `PRAGMA user_version`,
  one never-throw boundary), never a file in `server/migrations/`. A file whose `user_version` is newer than the code
  is unavailable.
- **Foreign keys inside `research.db`:** items → events `ON DELETE CASCADE`; `research_events.editor_key` →
  `editor_keys(editor_key)`, so **PR4 deletes an interval's events (and through them its items) before its key row**.
- **PR4's immediate deletion** (after a withdraw, and after a decline that withdraws) uses `getHandle()` from a
  service no file in `server/routes/` names `researchDb` from directly (Task 31 scans `server/routes/`), adds itself to
  `researchAppendOnly.test.js`'s `DELETE_ALLOWED` with its tables and its reason, and reads ids as `BigInt`. **PR4's
  scripts that read or delete** open the file themselves from `resolveResearchDbPath()` with `{ fileMustExist: true }`
  and `defaultSafeIntegers(true)`; **the paper-consent script may call `researchLog.ensureKey` and that deletion
  service**, whose `getHandle()` create path runs only while no `consented` row exists, so it is unreachable once one
  does.

### `server/services/researchEmission.js` (Tasks 19–26)

```ts
ITEM_SOURCES: Readonly<Record<ItemType, readonly (string|null)[]>>      // pinned against segment-save-state.js
CLAIM_VALIDATORS: Readonly<Record<ItemType, (item, at) => Claim>>       // G1.23 replaces the two suggestion entries;
                                                                        // at = place + offered(), memoized per call
NO_CLIENT; NONE                                                         // frozen field sets (decision 53)
ACCEPT_ELIGIBILITY_REFUSALS                                             // logged as write-refused (decision 49)
readClientFields(body) → { clientActionId: string|null, clientActionTime: number|null, replayed: boolean }
readAcceptOrigin(body) → 'single' | 'keyboard-stream' | null
readReviewedEditSha256(body) → string | null
claimAssistItems(raw, { book, chapter, moduleId, segmentId }) → { items: Item[], droppedCount: number }   // never throws
headFor(req, eventType, { book, chapter, moduleId }) → Head
emit(headFn, build) → void                    // never throws; record called bare, once; a builder throw → buildFailed
emitSave(req, { segmentId, editedContent, category, editorNote, saved })
emitRefused(req, { action: 'save' | 'accept' | 'propagate', segmentId, refusal, origin? })
emitAccept(req, { result })
emitReview(req, eventType, { edit, before })      // approve, reject, discuss, unapprove, return-to-pending
emitDelete(req, { before }); emitRevoke(req, { row, before }); emitComment(req, { edit, comment })
emitSubmit(req, { reviewId, stampedEditIds })
emitApply(req, { book, chapter, moduleId, before, trigger, reviewId? })
emitReviewComplete(req, { reviewId, result, notes, apply })
emitRestore(req, { version, before })
emitPropagation(req, { sourceSegmentId, sourceIs, latest, editedContent, category, note, writes, skipped })
emitModuleOpened(req, { detail })
```

**G1.23** calls `researchLog.record` for its own events (spec §8) and may build them through `emit`; it replaces the
two suggestion entries of `CLAIM_VALIDATORS` and adds no item type (§5.10's six are closed in `researchVocabulary.js`);
it adds its files to `featureFlags.ENV_SCAN_FILES` and its editing tables to `researchBoundary.test.js`'s
`FORBIDDEN_TABLES`.

### The before-images and write facts each service returns (the final shape)

| Function | Additive return |
|---|---|
| `saveSegmentEdit` | `before` (re-save, withdraw), `supersededEditIds`, `supersededAcceptanceIds` |
| `saveOnServedBase` | passes the three through (`before` `null` on insert and unchanged) |
| `supersedeForEdit` | `number[]` (was `undefined`) |
| `deleteSegmentEdit` | `{ deleted: true, before }` (was `true`) |
| `approveEdit`, `rejectEdit`, `markForDiscussion`, `unapproveEdit`, `returnEditToPending` | the row after, plus `before` |
| `revokeAcceptance` | the row after, plus `before` |
| `submitModuleForReview` | `stampedEditIds` |
| `addDiscussionComment` | `edit` |
| `applyApprovedEdits` | `before: { applied, superseded, selfHealReset: { edits, acceptances }, lapsedAcceptanceIds, stampedAcceptanceIds }` |
| `lapseDriftedIds` (new) | `number[]`; `lapseDrifted` still returns the count |
| `restoreVersion` (faithful track) | `before: { lapsedAcceptanceIds }` |
| `createPropagatedEdits` | `writes: [{ moduleId, segmentId, chapter, occurrence, editId, servedVersionId, inPlace, before, supersededAcceptanceIds }]` |
| `latestLiveEdit` (new) | `{ id, edited_content, editor_id } \| null`; `latestEditedText` unchanged |
| `SUPERSEDED_BY_NEWER_NOTE` (new export) | the fixed note an apply writes over a loser's `reviewer_note` |
| `concordanceService.suggestionFor` (new) | `{ is_text, book, chapter, module_id } \| null`, the repetition panel's own lookup |

Every route strips `before` and `writes` through `withoutResearchKeys` (`server/routes/segment-editor.js`) before
answering. PR4's withdraw-route deletion and PR5's exports read none of these (they read `research.db` and
`served_text_versions`).

## Before the PR

1. **The gate, as CI runs it, on the branch's tip** (index § Global Constraints): `npm test` from the repository root
   (diff the failing set by name against `main`'s, never the count); `npm run lint` **and** `npm run format:check`;
   `npm run docs:check` (Task 7 adds three route lines; expected clean once `docs/_generated/routes.md` is committed);
   `cd server && CI=1 npm run test:e2e` (routes change; the E2E database lists no approved text, so nothing is
   captured there, and the `rm` line clears `e2e-research.db`). No dependency is added. `git status --porcelain`
   empty.
2. **Re-check the migration number** (`ls server/migrations`) against `main` just before merging (index decision 9).
3. **A whole-branch adversarial review.** The newest practice is a blind pair, Opus and Fable on the same brief,
   neither seeing the other's findings; **[USER] commissioned the Fable half on 2026-11-04.** The alternative is a
   review workflow with several lenses and a skeptic per finding; a mutation pass restores from a golden `cp`, never
   `git checkout`, and `git status --porcelain` runs the moment any reviewing agent goes quiet. Fold confirmed
   findings in as their own commits. The Review Focus above is the brief's input-case list.
4. **The Icelandic strings** (decision 64) were approved as proposed on 2026-11-04, and the approval pass changed and
   added none. A string any later edit changes or adds goes to [USER] before the PR is opened (index decision 13).
5. **Push, open the PR (draft), and deploy only on [USER]'s go.** Merge as a **merge commit**, never squash or rebase.
   A push to `main` from dev puts prod's content backup behind until the next deploy (CLAUDE.md § Content delivery).
   The first deploy runs the old `deploy.sh`, so research events would say `git-head` until the second (spec §5.12);
   none is written anyway until a text is listed. After the deploy, `/api/health`'s `checks.research_db` should read
   `{ ok: true, reason: null }` (the file is created at boot while no consent exists).
6. A new top entry in the register: what merged, what was measured, what is next. **G1.23 may start once PR1–PR3 are
   on `main`** (index); consent collection waits for PR4 and its gates.

### Answers ([USER], 2026-11-04)

[USER] answered "Yes for all" to this plan's questions (index, "Answers"). The seven departures among them (decisions
9, 26, 48, 49, 54, 60, 65) are recorded in the spec's `✏️ AMENDED 2026-11-04` block, so no spec commit is owed before
the task that builds any of them.

- **Decision 9:** yes. A before-image never stores identity raw (the actor's own Entra id included) and keeps only the
  actor's own times.
- **Decision 26:** yes. `POST /api/research/consent` requires the text version the page showed, and answers 409
  `CONSENT_TEXT_CHANGED` when it is stale.
- **Decision 48:** yes. Apply's per-row outcomes (winners applied, losers superseded, the acceptances it stamps) live
  in the event's `detail`.
- **Decision 54:** yes. Propagation stores the client's text once (`content.text`, its hash in `detail`).
- **Decision 60:** yes. T11's apply runs on chemistry in a temp books root with the apply hold stubbed for that test.
- **Decision 16:** yes, as written. A replay with no client time is neither captured nor counted.
- **Decision 65:** yes, adopted. A saved or propagated text byte-identical to another editor's live edit is stored as
  its sha256 only; decision 65 says how, and Tasks 10, 11, 21, 25, 28 and 29 build and pin it.
- **Decision 25:** yes. PR3 ships the consent routes and no consent page; the page is G1.4's.
- **Decision 49:** yes, adopted. `SEGMENT_CONFLICT` and the accept's eligibility refusals (`NO_TRANSLATION`,
  `EDIT_EXISTS`, `DISCUSS_OPEN`, `HUMAN_CONTENT`) are logged as `write-refused`, with no hash; Tasks 21 and 22 build
  them.
- **Review Focus 12:** yes. The consent text must say that the request log shows a withdrawal was posted, and when
  (not by whom). That text is G1.4's: a dated note under G1.4 in the Skerfur plan carries it, and the register owns
  its status. Nothing in PR3 changes.
- **The Icelandic messages (decision 64):** approved as proposed.

## Handoff to PR4

Binding on PR4's plan ([deletion and backups](2026-11-03-g11-g12-pr4-deletion-and-backups.md)).

**PR4 may rely on:**
- **`research.db`'s schema and its keys:** `research_events` (random 63-bit `BigInt` ids, `UNIQUE (editor_key,
  key_seq)`, partial `UNIQUE (editor_key, client_action_id)`), `research_event_items` (`ON DELETE CASCADE` from its
  event), `editor_keys` (one row per interval; events reference it, so it is deleted **last**). An interval's rows are
  found by its key: `SELECT editor_key FROM editor_keys WHERE interval_id = ?`.
- **`research_consent`'s interval rule** (above): the withdraw route (Task 7) sets `withdrawn_at_ms` on the interval's
  starting row only and inserts nothing; `deletion_done_at_ms` exists and is never written by PR3. **PR4's immediate
  deletion runs after `researchConsent.withdraw` returns `withdrawn: true`, and after `recordDecision` returns
  `outcome: 'withdrew'`** (declining while consented, §5.14; its `intervalId` is the withdrawn interval's, Task 5),
  each after the commit, in the same route, through a service that never fails the request, and its result never
  changes the response (spec §5.15).
- **Scripts that read or delete research rows open the file themselves** (from `resolveResearchDbPath()`, with
  `{ fileMustExist: true }` and `defaultSafeIntegers(true)`). **The paper-consent script may call
  `researchLog.ensureKey` and PR4's deletion service**, which go through `researchDb.getHandle()`: its create path
  runs only while no `consented` row exists, so it is unreachable once a consent has been recorded (Task 6).
- **`research_capture_losses`** carries `interval_id` on every row but `consent-unknown` ones, so a withdrawal can delete
  an interval's loss rows by value; it has no id column (decision 19).
- **`researchDb.getHandle()`'s short wait** (`BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS`): a research write that meets PR4's `VACUUM` fails within
  it and is counted as a `busy` loss, never a stalled request (Tasks 6, 12).
- **The extension points:** `DELETE_ALLOWED` (Task 11; an entry is `{ tables, reason }` and allows only `DELETE FROM`
  a listed table, so PR4 adds entries and nothing else), `RESEARCH_SCRIPT_EXCEPTIONS` and a new `describe` in
  `researchBoundary.test.js` (Task 31), `EXPECTED_SCHEMA` (Task 3), `readResearchDbHealth`'s reasons (Task 13), and
  `researchConsent.recordDecision` / `withdraw` for the paper-consent script.
- **The next migration number in merge order** (predicted `056`).

**PR4 must not change:**
- `record`'s never-throw contract, its gate order (scope, identity, consent, replay rule, then build), or its loss
  rules: a loss row is written only for a consenting editor, one per `(interval, client_action_id)` (decisions 14–21);
- the research file's creation rule: created only by `fs.openSync(…, 0o600)` and only while no `consented` row exists,
  and never recreated by the server once one does (Task 6). A restore goes through PR4's admin script only (§5.15);
- the write-only surface of `researchLog` (decision 40; PR4's deletion lives in its own service or script, beside it);
- `checks.research_db`'s two-key shape and its "no count" rule (decision 37);
- the field classes of `researchFilter.BEFORE_FIELD_CLASSES` without a decision and the drift guard's agreement;
- migration `055` and research schema step 1 (a change is a new migration or a new step).

**Whoever builds re-attach by content (10-10 plan 4.2; not PR4, not PR5)** owes spec §5.9's sentence "Re-attach
emits through the same call, with the edit's author as actor and origin `re-attach`", which no path in this project
can reach (index decision 7: the id-based script refuses the study book). A script has no Express `req`, so it cannot
use `researchEmission`'s `emit` or `headFor`; it calls `researchLog.record` directly, bare, after its write commits,
with `actorProviderId` the edit's author's provider id (the row's `editor_id`, decision 8), `eventType 'edit-save'`,
`origin: 're-attach'` (PR1's `EDIT_ORIGINS`), `servedVersionId` the version its `saveOnServedBase({ …, origin:
're-attach', requireEcho: false })` minted, and `content` / `detail` by the `edit-save` rules (decision 51), with no
`clientActionId`. The consent gate then decides as for anyone else, and `record` applies decision 65 itself (a text
another editor holds live is stored as its hash), so the script passes the text as for any save.

**PR5 may rely on** `bookSwitches.firstSwitchOnMs` and PR2's `module_openings` for `opening-eligibility.js` (server-side
only; the output holds book, chapter, module and the yes/no), and on `served_text_versions` joined by value; it reads no
research table except through PR4's admin reader (§5.19's last bullet).
